Static-analysis context for repository-level code navigation. Use the source links to verify details. Call edges are analyzer-resolved relationships, not complete runtime traces.

## Analyzer Architecture Summary

- modelmesh-serving (also known as\: ModelMesh) is a Kubernetes component.
- managing 4 CRD(s)\: ClusterServingRuntime, InferenceService, Predictor, ServingRuntime.
- deploying 4 workload(s) (controller-manager, modelmesh-controller, template-value-template-value, etcd) with 6 container(s).
- watching 11 resource type(s).
- exposing 3 service(s) on ports\: 2379, 8080, 9443.
- with 3 webhook(s) (1 validating).
- protected by 4 network policy/policies.
- connecting to external services\: azure-blob.
- runtime dependencies\: MinIO/S3.
- defining 2 API spec type(s) across 12 struct(s) with 43 total fields.
- with 279 operator config constants (3 env vars).
- following 1 reconciliation steps across 1 controller(s).
- integrates with Prometheus, management relationship via labels/annotations.
- deploys OAuth proxy sidecar for authentication.
- deploys kube-rbac-proxy sidecar for RBAC-based authorization.
- kustomize overlays\: config/certmanager, config/crd, config/default, config/manager, config/namespace-runtimes, config/overlays/odh/crd, config/overlays/odh/default, config/overlays/odh, config/overlays/odh/manager, config/overlays/odh/rbac, config/overlays/odh/scripts, config/overlays/odh/webhook, config/prometheus, config/rbac/cluster-scope, config/rbac/common, config/rbac/namespace-scope, config/runtimes, config/webhook.
- references components\: kserve (import).
- mounts 2 ConfigMap(s) as volumes\: etcd-scripts, model-serving-config-defaults.
